       CONFIGURATION SECTION.

               PRINTER IS PRINTER   
               CONSOLE IS CONSOLE

162857         SWITCH-24 IS ERROR-SW, ON STATUS IS IN-ERROR,
162857                               OFF STATUS IS NO-ERROR.

       INPUT-OUTPUT SECTION.
       FILE-CONTROL.
           SELECT BIG-BUCKET      ASSIGN TO DISC.   
           SELECT BIG-CONTINGENCY-BUCKET
                                  ASSIGN TO DISC SHOREXSC01D.   
           SELECT RUN-FILE        ASSIGN TO DISC RUNFILE.   
           SELECT CARD-FILE       ASSIGN TO DISC DPBVOY01C. 
           SELECT RATE-CARD-FILE  ASSIGN TO DISC DPBYOY01D  
                                  ORGANIZATION IS INDEXED   
                                  ACCESS MODE IS DYNAMIC
                                  RECORD KEY IS RCF-KEY.

162857        FILE STATUS IS WS-IF-FILE-STATUS.

144449        ERROR RECOVERY IS 9100-DATABASE-ERROR 
144449        ROLLBACK       IS 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.

862319     EXEC SQL WHENEVER SQLERROR   
862319          GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR   
862319     END-EXEC.
PPC470     EXEC SQL WHENEVER NOT FOUND CONTINUE END-EXEC.   

       1000-MAIN-PARA.  
           COPY IRP48INT.   
           IF  WS-RESTART-SWITCH = 'N'  
               PERFORM 1010-OPEN-PARA THRU  
                       1010-OPEN-PARA-EXIT  
               PERFORM 1020-READ-CARD-FILE THRU 
                       1020-READ-CARD-FILE-EXIT 
191395         PERFORM 1022-READ-CHARTER-FILE THRU  
191395                 1022-READ-CHARTER-FILE-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM 1030-RB-RESTART THRU 
                   1030-RB-RESTART-EXIT.
           PERFORM 1040-FETCH-SAIL1-REC THRU
                   1040-FETCH-SAIL1-REC-EXIT.   

191395     IF WS-COLOUR-FLAG = 'Y'  
191395        PERFORM 1025-INITIALIZE-TABLE THRU
191395                1025-INITIALIZE-TABLE-EXIT
191395        PERFORM 1025-CRT-INX-BKNG-TABLE THRU  
191395                1025-CRT-INX-BKNG-TABLE-EXIT  
191395     END-IF.  

           PERFORM 1050-CONTROL-PARA THRU   
                   1050-CONTROL-PARA-EXIT.  

       1010-OPEN-PARA.  
162857     MOVE ZERO              TO  WS-J  
162857                                WS-RECORDS-READ.  
162857     MOVE 'N'               TO  WS-EOF-FLAG.  
           SET ERROR-SW                TO OFF.  
           ACCEPT IN-PARAM FROM CARD-READER.
           OPEN INPUT  CARD-FILE
191395                 SS-COLOUR-INX-FILE   
191395                 CHARTER-CARD-FILE
191395          OUTPUT RATE-CARD-FILE   
191395                 REPORT-FILE. 
162857     OPEN INPUT ITEM-DP-FILE. 
162857     IF IF-FILE-OK
162857        NEXT SENTENCE 
162857     ELSE 
162857        DISPLAY 'I-O ERROR ITEM-DP-FILE FILE STATUS = '   
162857           WS-IF-FILE-STATUS UPON PRINTER 
162857        GO TO 9100-I-O-FILE-ERROR.

162857* SKIP THE FIRST RECORD IN THE INPUT FILE AS IT CONTAINS FIELD
162857* NAMES.  

162857     READ ITEM-DP-FILE AT END 
162857          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.
162857     IF IF-FILE-OK
162857        NEXT SENTENCE 
162857     ELSE 
162857        IF IF-FILE-AT-END 
162857           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
162857        ELSE  
162857           DISPLAY 'I-O ERROR ITEM-DP-FILE FILE STATUS = '
162857                   WS-IF-FILE-STATUS UPON PRINTER 
162857           GO TO 9100-I-O-FILE-ERROR. 

162857     PERFORM 9050-READ-IF-PARA THRU 9050-READ-IF-EXIT 
162857        UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'.  

       1010-OPEN-PARA-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

       1020-READ-CARD-FILE. 

           READ CARD-FILE NEXT  
              AT END GO TO 1021-END-RATE-CARD-CREATION. 

           IF (CR-PAX-PRICE-CODE = SPACES) OR   
              (CR-PAX-PRICE-TYPE = SPACES)  
              GO TO 1020-READ-CARD-FILE.

           MOVE SPACES                 TO RATE-CARD-REC.
           MOVE CR-PAX-FARE-CODE       TO RCF-PAX-FARE-CODE.
           MOVE CR-PAX-PRICE-TYPE      TO RCF-PAX-PRICE-TYPE.   
           MOVE CR-PAX-PRICE-CODE      TO RCF-PAX-PRICE-CODE.   
           MOVE CR-DESC                TO RCF-DESC. 
           WRITE RATE-CARD-REC  
               INVALID KEY GO TO 1020-READ-CARD-FILE.   

           ADD 1                       TO WS-RCF-COUNT. 
           GO TO 1020-READ-CARD-FILE.   

       1021-END-RATE-CARD-CREATION. 

           IF  WS-RCF-COUNT = ZEROES
               GO TO 9300-NO-RATE-CARD-ERROR
           END-IF.  

           CLOSE CARD-FILE  
                 RATE-CARD-FILE.
           MOVE IN-CSF-FILE-QUALIFIER  TO FILE-QUAL 
                                          CSF-QUAL  
                                          CSFA-QUAL.

       1020-READ-CARD-FILE-EXIT.
           EXIT.

191395 1022-READ-CHARTER-FILE.  
191395     READ CHARTER-CARD-FILE   
191395          AT END GO TO 1023-COLOUR-CODING.
191395     ADD  1                      TO WS-READ-CNT.  
191395     MOVE CC-SHIP-CODE           TO WS-SHIP-CODE. 
191395     MOVE CC-SAIL-DATE           TO WS-SAIL-DATE. 
191395     MOVE CC-DURATION-DAYS       TO WS-DURATION-DAYS. 
191395     DISPLAY 'READ CHARTER FILE' UPON PRINTER.
191395     GO TO 1022-READ-CHARTER-FILE-EXIT.   

191395 1023-COLOUR-CODING.  
191395     IF  WS-READ-CNT = ZERO   
191395         DISPLAY 'CHARTER FILE IS EMPTY' UPON PRINTER 
191395     END-IF.  

191395 1022-READ-CHARTER-FILE-EXIT. 
191395     EXIT.

191395 1025-INITIALIZE-TABLE.   
191395     MOVE SPACES                 TO WS-BKG-INDEX-TABLE.   
191395     MOVE 0                      TO WS-BOOKING-COUNT. 
191395     STRING 'PAX-BOOK-NUM,PAX-CABIN-NUM,PAX-SEQ-NUM,' 
191395            'PAX-CABIN-SEQ-NUM,PAX-CABIN-BOOK-NUM,'   
191395            'PAX-SHIP-CODE,PAX-SAIL-DATE' 
191395            DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO WS-REPORT-REC. 

191395     WRITE REPORT-REC FROM WS-REPORT-REC. 

191395 1025-INITIALIZE-TABLE-EXIT.  
191395     EXIT.

191395 1025-CRT-INX-BKNG-TABLE. 

191395     READ SS-COLOUR-INX-FILE NEXT RECORD  
191395          AT END GO TO 1025-CRT-INX-BKNG-TABLE-EXIT.  

191395     SET WS-BKG-INX-KEY          TO 1.
191395     SEARCH WS-BKG-REC
191395        AT END
191395           ADD 1                 TO WS-BOOKING-COUNT  
191395           SET WS-BKG-INX-KEY    TO WS-BOOKING-COUNT  
191395           MOVE SCIF-PAX-BOOK-NUM 
191395                                 TO WS-BOOK1-NUM( WS-BKG-INX-KEY )
191395        WHEN WS-BOOK1-NUM ( WS-BKG-INX-KEY ) = SCIF-PAX-BOOK-NUM  
191395           NEXT SENTENCE. 
191395     GO TO 1025-CRT-INX-BKNG-TABLE.   

191395 1025-CRT-INX-BKNG-TABLE-EXIT.
191395     EXIT.

       1030-RB-RESTART. 

           IMPART.  
           OPEN ALL USAGE-MODE IS RETRIEVAL.

           IF  WS-RESTART-SW NOT = 'Y'  
               OPEN OUTPUT RUN-FILE 
                    OUTPUT BIG-CONTINGENCY-BUCKET   
                    INPUT  RATE-CARD-FILE   
           END-IF.  

           IF  DO-ALL-DATES 
               ACCEPT TODAYS-DATE FROM DATE YYYYMMDD
               MOVE TODAYS-DATE        TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE   
               PERFORM DPP10-YYYYMMDD-TO-MMDDYYYY   
               MOVE DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE 
                                       TO DPP02-GREGORIAN-DATE  
               PERFORM DPP10-GREGORIAN-TO-JULIAN
               MOVE 21                 TO DPP02-NUMBER-DAYS 
               PERFORM DPP10-ADVANCE-JULIAN-DATE
               MOVE DPP02-JULIAN-DATE  TO FORTNIGHT 
               PERFORM DPP10-JULIAN-TO-GREGORIAN
           ELSE 
PPC470*        MOVE IN-SAIL-DATE       TO SAIL1-SAIL-DATE   
PPC470         MOVE IN-SAIL-DATE       TO TDP01-I-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD
                                          DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE   
               PERFORM DPP10-YYYYMMDD-TO-MMDDYYYY   
               MOVE DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE 
                                       TO DPP02-GREGORIAN-DATE  
               PERFORM DPP10-GREGORIAN-TO-JULIAN
               MOVE DPP02-JULIAN-DATE  TO FORTNIGHT 
               PERFORM DPP10-JULIAN-TO-GREGORIAN
           END-IF.  

           MOVE LOW-VALUES             TO COUNTS.   

           IF  DO-ALL-DATES 
               IF  DO-ALL-SHIPS 
                   DISPLAY 'WILL CREATE FILES FOR ALL SHIPS & VOYAGES ' 
                       'THRU ' FORTNIGHT UPON CONSOLE   
               ELSE 
                   DISPLAY 'WILL CREATE FILES FOR SHIP ' IN-SHIP-CODE   
                       ' & ALL VOYAGES THRU ' FORTNIGHT UPON CONSOLE
               END-IF   
           ELSE 
               IF  DO-ALL-SHIPS 
                   DISPLAY 'WILL CREATE FILES FOR ALL SHIPS & ' 
                       ' ONLY FOR JULIAN-DATE ' DPP02-GREGORIAN-DATE
                       UPON CONSOLE 
               ELSE 
                   DISPLAY 'WILL CREATE FILES FOR SHIP ' IN-SHIP-CODE   
                       ' ONLY FOR JULIAN-DATE ' DPP02-GREGORIAN-DATE
                       UPON CONSOLE 
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

       1030-RB-RESTART-EXIT.
           EXIT.

       1040-FETCH-SAIL1-REC.
PPC470*    IF  DO-ALL-DATES 
PPC470*        MOVE TODAYS-DATE        TO SAIL1-SAIL-DATE   
PPC470*    END-IF.  

PPC470*    IF  WS-RESTART-SW = 'Y'

PPC470*    END-IF.

PPC470*        MOVE SPACES             TO SAIL1-SHIP-CODE   
PPC470*    ELSE 
PPC470*        MOVE IN-SHIP-CODE       TO SAIL1-SHIP-CODE   
PPC470*    END-IF.  
PPC470* 
PPC470*    MOVE ZEROES                 TO SAIL1-DURATION-DAYS.  
PPC470*    MOVE 'SAIL1-FETCH'          TO PROBLEM-FETCH.
PPC470*    MOVE ZEROES                 TO ERROR-NUM.

PPC470*    IF  IN-DURATION-DAYS <= 0
PPC470*        MOVE ZEROES             TO SAIL1-DURATION-DAYS   
PPC470*    ELSE 
PPC470*        MOVE IN-DURATION-DAYS   TO SAIL1-DURATION-DAYS   
PPC470*    END-IF.  

PPC470*    FETCH SAIL1-REC RECORD   
PPC470*       ON ERROR GO TO 1041-FSAIL-ERROR.  

PPC470*    IF  (WS-READ-CNT > 0 )   
PPC470*        IF  ( WS-SHIP-CODE = SAIL1-SHIP-CODE AND 
PPC470*              WS-SAIL-DATE = SAIL1-SAIL-DATE AND 
PPC470*              WS-DURATION-DAYS = SAIL1-DURATION-DAYS )   
PPC470*              MOVE 'Y'           TO WS-COLOUR-FLAG   
PPC470*        ELSE 
PPC470*              MOVE 'N'           TO WS-COLOUR-FLAG   
PPC470*        END-IF   
PPC470*    END-IF.  

PPC470*    IF  ((NOT DO-ALL-SHIPS)                   AND
PPC470*        (SAIL1-SHIP-CODE NOT = IN-SHIP-CODE)) OR 
PPC470*        (SAIL1-RECAT-CODE = 'Y') 
PPC470*        PERFORM UNTIL (SAIL1-SHIP-CODE = IN-SHIP-CODE)  AND  
PPC470*            (SAIL1-STATUS-CODE NOT = 'C' AND 'D' AND 'W')
PPC470*            FETCH NEXT WITHIN SAIL1-REC RECORD   
PPC470*            AT END        GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB  
PPC470*            ON ERROR      GO TO 1041-FSAIL-ERROR 
PPC470*        END-PERFORM  
PPC470*    ELSE 
PPC470*        IF  DO-ALL-SHIPS OR  
PPC470*            (SAIL1-RECAT-CODE = 'Y') 
PPC470*            PERFORM  
PPC470*               UNTIL SAIL1-STATUS-CODE NOT = 'C' AND 'D' AND 'W' 
PPC470*               FETCH NEXT WITHIN SAIL1-REC RECORD
PPC470*                  AT END      GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB  
PPC470*                  ON ERROR    GO TO 1041-FSAIL-ERROR 
PPC470*            END-PERFORM  
PPC470*        ELSE 
PPC470*            PERFORM UNTIL
PPC470*                (SAIL1-STATUS-CODE NOT = 'C' AND 'D' AND 'W') AND
PPC470*                (SAIL1-SHIP-CODE = IN-SHIP-CODE) 
PPC470*                FETCH NEXT WITHIN SAIL1-REC RECORD   
PPC470*                AT END      GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
PPC470*                ON ERROR    GO TO 1041-FSAIL-ERROR   
PPC470*            END-PERFORM  
PPC470*        END-IF   
PPC470*    END-IF.  
PPC470*    IF  (WS-READ-CNT > 0 )   
PPC470*        IF  ( WS-SHIP-CODE = SAIL1-SHIP-CODE AND 
PPC470*              WS-SAIL-DATE = SAIL1-SAIL-DATE AND 
PPC470*              WS-DURATION-DAYS = SAIL1-DURATION-DAYS )   
PPC470*              MOVE 'Y'           TO WS-COLOUR-FLAG   
PPC470*        ELSE 
PPC470*              MOVE 'N'           TO WS-COLOUR-FLAG   
PPC470*        END-IF   
PPC470*    END-IF.  
PPC470* 
PPC470*    MOVE SAIL1-SAIL-DATE        TO WSSL1-SAIL-DATE.  

PPC470     IF  DO-ALL-DATES 
PPC470         MOVE TODAYS-DATE        TO TDP01-I-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     IF  WS-RESTART-SW = 'Y'  
PPC470         GO TO 1042-RESTART-RUN   
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     IF  DO-ALL-SHIPS 
PPC470         MOVE SPACES             TO TDP01-I-SHIP-CODE 
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470         MOVE IN-SHIP-CODE       TO TDP01-I-SHIP-CODE 
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     MOVE ZEROES                 TO TDP01-I-DUR-DAYS. 

PPC470     IF  IN-DURATION-DAYS <= 0
PPC470         MOVE ZEROES             TO TDP01-I-DUR-DAYS  
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470         MOVE IN-DURATION-DAYS   TO TDP01-I-DUR-DAYS  
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     PERFORM TDP01-FETCH-FIRST-SAIL THRU  
PPC470             TDP01-FETCH-SAIL-EXIT.   

PPC470     IF TDP01-SAIL-SQLERR OR  
PPC470        TDP01-SAIL-NOTFND OR  
PPC470        TDP01-PROC-ERROR > ZEROES 
PPC470        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     IF TDP01-SCAT-SAIL-ID = TDP01-SAIL-ID AND
PPC470        TDP01-SAIL-CAT-CNT > 1
PPC470        CONTINUE  
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470        PERFORM TDP01-RET-ALL-SCAT THRU   
PPC470                TDP01-RET-ALL-SCAT-EXIT   
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     IF  ((NOT DO-ALL-SHIPS)                   AND
PPC470         (TDP01-SHIP-CODE NOT = IN-SHIP-CODE)) OR 
PPC470         (TDP01-RECAT-CODE = 'Y') 
PPC470             PERFORM UNTIL (TDP01-SHIP-CODE = IN-SHIP-CODE)  AND  
PPC470                 (TDP01-STATUS-CODE NOT = 'C' AND 'D' AND 'W')
PPC470                  PERFORM TDP01-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL THRU  
PPC470                         TDP01-FETCH-SAIL-EXIT
PPC470                  IF TDP01-SAIL-SQLERR
PPC470                     GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR
PPC470                  END-IF  
PPC470             END-PERFORM  
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470         IF  DO-ALL-SHIPS OR  
PPC470             (TDP01-RECAT-CODE = 'Y') 
PPC470               PERFORM
PPC470                UNTIL TDP01-STATUS-CODE NOT = 'C' AND 'D' AND 'W' 
PPC470                  PERFORM TDP01-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL THRU  
PPC470                          TDP01-FETCH-SAIL-EXIT   
PPC470                  IF TDP01-SAIL-SQLERR
PPC470                     GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR
PPC470                  END-IF  
PPC470             END-PERFORM  
PPC470         ELSE 
PPC470             PERFORM UNTIL
PPC470                 (TDP01-STATUS-CODE NOT = 'C' AND 'D' AND 'W') AND
PPC470                 (TDP01-SHIP-CODE = IN-SHIP-CODE) 
PPC470                  PERFORM TDP01-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL THRU  
PPC470                          TDP01-FETCH-SAIL-EXIT   
PPC470                  IF TDP01-SAIL-SQLERR
PPC470                     GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR
PPC470                  END-IF  
PPC470             END-PERFORM  
PPC470         END-IF   
PPC470     END-IF.  
PPC470     IF  (WS-READ-CNT > 0 )   
PPC470         IF  ( WS-SHIP-CODE = TDP01-SHIP-CODE AND 
PPC470               WS-SAIL-DATE = TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD AND
PPC470               WS-DURATION-DAYS = TDP01-DURATION-DAYS )   
PPC470               MOVE 'Y'           TO WS-COLOUR-FLAG   
PPC470         ELSE 
PPC470               MOVE 'N'           TO WS-COLOUR-FLAG   
PPC470         END-IF   
PPC470     END-IF.  
PPC470  
PPC470     MOVE TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD
PPC470                                 TO WSSL1-SAIL-DATE.  

           GO TO 1040-FETCH-SAIL1-REC-EXIT.

PPC470*

PPC470*        DISPLAY '* SAIL1-REC NOT FOUND FOR '        UPON CONSOLE 
PPC470*        DISPLAY '*     SHIP-CODE: ' SAIL1-SHIP-CODE UPON CONSOLE 
PPC470*        DISPLAY '*     SAIL-DATE: ' SAIL1-SAIL-DATE UPON CONSOLE 
PPC470*        DISPLAY SPACES UPON PRINTER  
PPC470*        GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR
PPC470*    END-IF.  

       1042-RESTART-RUN.

PPC470*    MOVE CSF-DAYS               TO SAIL1-DURATION-DAYS.

PPC470*        IF ( WS-SHIP-CODE = SAIL1-SHIP-CODE AND  
PPC470*             WS-SAIL-DATE = SAIL1-SAIL-DATE AND  
PPC470*             WS-DURATION-DAYS = SAIL1-DURATION-DAYS )
PPC470*             MOVE 'Y'           TO WS-COLOUR-FLAG
PPC470*        ELSE 
PPC470*             MOVE 'N'           TO WS-COLOUR-FLAG
PPC470*        END-IF   
PPC470*    END-IF.  

PPC470     MOVE CSF-DATE               TO TDP01-I-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD.   
PPC470     MOVE CSF-SHIP               TO TDP01-I-SHIP-CODE.
PPC470     MOVE CSF-DAYS               TO TDP01-I-DUR-DAYS. 

PPC470     PERFORM TDP01-FETCH-FIRST-SAIL THRU  
PPC470             TDP01-FETCH-SAIL-EXIT.   

PPC470     IF TDP01-SAIL-SQLERR OR  
PPC470        TDP01-SAIL-NOTFND OR  
PPC470        TDP01-PROC-ERROR > ZEROES 
PPC470        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     IF TDP01-SCAT-SAIL-ID = TDP01-SAIL-ID AND
PPC470        TDP01-SAIL-CAT-CNT > 1
PPC470        CONTINUE  
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470        PERFORM TDP01-RET-ALL-SCAT THRU   
PPC470                TDP01-RET-ALL-SCAT-EXIT   
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     IF  (WS-READ-CNT > 0 )   
PPC470         IF  ( WS-SHIP-CODE = TDP01-SHIP-CODE AND 
PPC470               WS-SAIL-DATE = TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD AND
PPC470               WS-DURATION-DAYS = TDP01-DURATION-DAYS )   
PPC470               MOVE 'Y'          TO WS-COLOUR-FLAG
PPC470         ELSE 
PPC470               MOVE 'N'          TO WS-COLOUR-FLAG
PPC470         END-IF   
PPC470     END-IF.  
           MOVE ' '                    TO WS-RESTART-SW.

       1050-CONTROL-PARA.   

           MOVE WSSL1-SAIL-DATE        TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE.  
           PERFORM DPP10-YYYYMMDD-TO-MMDDYYYY.  
           MOVE DPP02-MMDDYYYY-DATE    TO DPP02-GREGORIAN-DATE. 
           PERFORM DPP10-GREGORIAN-TO-JULIAN.   

           IF  DPP02-JULIAN-DATE > FORTNIGHT
               GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM 2100-SET-UP-BIG-BUCKET THRU  
                   2100-SET-UP-BIG-BUCKET-EXIT. 
           PERFORM 3100-MOVE-SAIL1 THRU 
                   3100-MOVE-SAIL1-EXIT.
CCLCOM     PERFORM 4000-GET-HYB-SAIL-FILE THRU  
CCLCOM             4000-HYB-SAIL-FILE-EXIT. 
           PERFORM 4100-PROCESS-IBR-PAX THRU
                   4100-PROCESS-IBR-PAX-EXIT.   
           PERFORM 5100-PROCESS-GBR-PAX THRU
                   5100-PROCESS-GBR-PAX-EXIT.   
           PERFORM 6100-AVAIL-CABIN THRU
                   6100-AVAIL-CABIN-EXIT.   
180675     PERFORM 6500-IMAX-PORTS THRU 
180675             6500-IMAX-PORTS-EXIT.
           PERFORM 6600-ITINERARY THRU  
                   6600-ITINERARY-EXIT. 
           PERFORM 7100-GROUP-AGENT THRU
                   7100-GROUP-AGENT-EXIT
               VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
               UNTIL SUB > G-AGENT-TABLE-SIZE.  
           PERFORM 7600-INDV-AGENT THRU 
                   7600-INDV-AGENT-EXIT 
               VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
               UNTIL SUB > I-AGENT-TABLE-SIZE.  
           PERFORM 8100-NATIONALITY-REC THRU
                   8100-NATIONALITY-REC-EXIT
               VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
               UNTIL NR-CODE (SUB) = SPACES       OR
                                     ZEROES       OR
                               SUB > NR-TABLE-SIZE. 
160400* NEW RECORD TYPES
160400     PERFORM 8200-VCR THRU 8200-VCR-EXIT. 
160400     PERFORM 8300-CITIES THRU 8300-CITIES-EXIT.   
160400     PERFORM 8400-CARRIER THRU 8400-CARRIER-EXIT. 
184822     PERFORM 6500-CDER-LIST THRU  
184822             6500-CDER-LIST-EXIT. 
           PERFORM 8600-NEW-DATE-PARA THRU  
                  8600-NEW-DATE-PARA-EXIT.  

           IF  WS-CALL-CONTROL-PARA = 'Y'   
               GO TO 1050-CONTROL-PARA

       1050-CONTROL-PARA-EXIT.

       2100-SET-UP-BIG-BUCKET.  
PPC470*    MOVE SAIL1-SHIP-CODE        TO CSF-SHIP  
PPC470*                                   CSFA-SHIP.
PPC470*    MOVE SAIL1-SAIL-DATE        TO CSF-DATE  
PPC470*                                   CSFA-DATE.

PPC470*                                   CSFA-DAYS.

PPC470                                    CSFA-SHIP.

PPC470                                 TO CSF-DATE  
PPC470                                    CSFA-DATE.
PPC470     MOVE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS    TO CSF-DAYS  
PPC470                                    CSFA-DAYS.
           MOVE SPACES                 TO FILE-FILE.
           STRING CSF-SHIP, CSF-DATE, CSF-DAYS  
               DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO FILE-FILE.

           PERFORM 2110-CHECK-IF-CATALOGUED THRU
                   2110-CHECK-CATALOGUED-EXIT.  

           IF  IS-CATALOGUED
               CALL 'ERACSF' USING CSF-ASGA-IMAGE   
                                   CSF-STATUS   
           ELSE 
               CALL 'ERACSF' USING CSF-ASG-IMAGE
                                   CSF-STATUS   
           END-IF.  

           DISPLAY 'CSF-ASG-IMAGE - ' CSF-STATUS       UPON PRINTER.
           IF  CSF-STATUS NOT = 0   
               IF  NOT IS-CATALOGUED
                   DISPLAY 'PROBLEM ASSIGNING FILE '
                                             CSF-ASG2  UPON PRINTER 
                   GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
               ELSE 
                   DISPLAY 'PROBLEM ASSIGNING FILE '
                                             CSF-ASGA2 UPON PRINTER 
                   GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  


           IF  IS-CATALOGUED
               MOVE CSF-FILEA-NAME     TO CSF-USE2  
           ELSE 
               MOVE CSF-FILE-NAME      TO CSF-USE2  
           END-IF.  

           CALL 'ERACSF' USING CSF-USE-IMAGE, CSF-STATUS.   
           DISPLAY CSF-USE-IMAGE '-' CSF-STATUS           UPON PRINTER. 

           IF  CSF-STATUS NOT = 0   
               DISPLAY 'PROBLEM ON @USE F: ' CSF-USE-IMAGE UPON PRINTER 
               GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
           END-IF.  

           OPEN OUTPUT BIG-BUCKET.  
           MOVE 'O'                    TO BIG-BUCKET-SW.
           MOVE ZEROES                 TO SAIL-COUNT
                                          IBR-COUNT 
                                          GBR-COUNT 
                                          PAX-COUNT 
                                          AVAIL-COUNT   
                                          AGENT-COUNT   
                                          NR-COUNT  
                                          ITR-COUNT 
                                          CODR-COUNT
                                          COMR-COUNT
                                          EDR-COUNT 
                                          ESDR-COUNT
                                          EOR-COUNT 
                                          EGOR-COUNT
                                          CONT-COUNT
                                          G-PAX-CTR 
                                          I-PAX-CTR.

214948*    MOVE ZEROES                 TO I-AGENT-TABLE 
214948     MOVE ZEROES                 TO G-AGENT-TABLE.
           MOVE SPACES                 TO NR-TABLE  
                                          AV-TABLE  
214948                                    I-AGENT-TABLE.
           MOVE 1                      TO G-AGENT-TABLE-SIZE
                                          I-AGENT-TABLE-SIZE
                                          AV-TABLE-SIZE 
                                          NR-TABLE-SIZE.
           MOVE SPACES                 TO BIG-BUCKET-REC.   
           ACCEPT RUN-DATE FROM DATE YYYYMMDD.  
           ACCEPT RUN-TIME FROM TIME.   
           MOVE RUN-MO                 TO  H-MO.
           MOVE RUN-DY                 TO  H-DY.
           MOVE RUN-YR                 TO  H-YR.
           MOVE RUN-HH                 TO  RED-HH.  
           MOVE RUN-MM                 TO  RED-MM.  
           MOVE RED-TIME               TO  H-TIME.  
           MOVE 0001                   TO  H-SIZE1. 
           MOVE H-FIRST-LINE           TO  BBR-TOP. 
           MOVE 14                     TO  BBR-SIZE.
           MOVE PGM-VERSION            TO  BBR-REC. 
           INSPECT BIG-BUCKET-REC REPLACING ALL LOW-VALUES BY SPACES.   
           WRITE BIG-BUCKET-REC.
           MOVE 1                      TO BIG-BUCKET-REC-CNT.   

       2100-SET-UP-BIG-BUCKET-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

       2110-CHECK-IF-CATALOGUED.

           MOVE 'N'                    TO WS-CATALOGUED-SW. 
           MOVE 1                      TO WS-SAVE-BF-SUB.   
           PERFORM 2120-SEARCH THRU 2120-SEARCH-EXIT
               UNTIL IS-CATALOGUED OR   
                     WS-SAVE-BF-SUB > WS-TOTAL-BF.  

       2110-CHECK-CATALOGUED-EXIT.  
           EXIT.

       2120-SEARCH. 

           IF  FILE-QUAL = WS-SAVE-BF-QUAL (WS-SAVE-BF-SUB)   AND   
               FILE-FILE = WS-SAVE-BF-FILE-NAME (WS-SAVE-BF-SUB)
               MOVE 'Y'                TO WS-CATALOGUED-SW  
           ELSE 
               ADD 1                   TO WS-SAVE-BF-SUB
           END-IF.  

       2120-SEARCH-EXIT.
           EXIT.

      ******************************************************************
      *TO MOVE SAIL1-REC FIELDS TO CORRESPONDING WSSL1-REC FIELDS.     *
      ******************************************************************

       3100-MOVE-SAIL1. 

           MOVE SPACES                 TO BIG-BUCKET-REC.   
PPC470*    MOVE SAIL1-SHIP-CODE        TO BBR-SHIP. 
PPC470*    MOVE SAIL1-SAIL-DATE        TO BBR-DATE. 
PPC470*    MOVE SAIL1-DURATION-DAYS    TO BBR-DAYS.

PPC470*    MOVE 7567                   TO BBR-SIZE.

PPC470 3700-BUILD-PRC-FRM-RTE.  

PPC470     EXEC SQL 
PPC470          SELECT  PHASE_NUM,  
PPC470                  RATE_CODE   
PPC470             INTO :WS-PRICE-PHASE-NUM,
PPC470                  :WS-RATE-CODE   
PPC470            FROM  RATE_MASTER 
PPC470           WHERE  PRP_OWNER_ID   = :WS-RTE-OWN-ID AND 
PPC470                  PRP_OWNER_CODE = 'RTE'  
PPC470           FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY 
PPC470     END-EXEC.
PPC470     IF SQLCODE < 0   
PPC470         GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470        IF SQLCODE = 100  
PPC470           MOVE SPACES    TO WS-RATE-CODE 
PPC470           MOVE ZEROES    TO WS-PRICE-PHASE-NUM   
PPC470        END-IF
PPC470     END-IF.  
PPC470     MOVE WS-PRICE-PHASE-NUM         TO WSPAX-PRICE-PHASE-NUM.
PPC470     MOVE WS-RATE-CODE (2:2)         TO WSPAX-PRICE-CODE. 
PPC470     MOVE WS-RATE-CODE (1:1)         TO WSPAX-PRICE-TYPE. 

PPC470 3700-BUILD-PRC-FRM-RTE-EXIT. 
PPC470     EXIT.

PPC470 3800-BUILD-PRC-FRM-ADB.  

PPC470     EXEC SQL 
PPC470          SELECT PHASE_NUM,   
PPC470                 ADD_BENEFIT_CODE 
PPC470            INTO :WS-PRICE-PHASE-NUM, 
PPC470                 :WS-OWNER-CODE   
PPC470            FROM  ADD_BENEFIT_MASTER  
PPC470            WHERE PRP_OWNER_ID   = :WS-ADB-OWN-ID 
PPC470            AND   PRP_OWNER_CODE = 'ADB'  
PPC470            AND   ADD_BENEFIT_TYPE = 'E'  
PPC470           FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY 
PPC470     END-EXEC 
PPC470     IF SQLCODE < 0   
PPC470         GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470        IF SQLCODE = 100  
PPC470           MOVE SPACES TO WS-OWNER-CODE   
PPC470           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-PRICE-PHASE-NUM  
PPC470        END-IF
PPC470     END-IF.

CCLCOM 4000-GET-HYB-SAIL-FILE.  
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     MOVE 'N'                     TO WS-HYB-FILE-FOUND.   
CCLCOM     PERFORM 4050-ASG-FILE      THRU 4050-ASG-FILE-EXIT.  
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     IF WS-HYB-FILE-FOUND = 'N'   
CCLCOM        GO TO 4000-HYB-SAIL-FILE-EXIT.
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     SORT SORT-HYB-FILE ON
CCLCOM          ASCENDING KEY SORT-HYB-BOOK-NUM 
CCLCOM                        SORT-HYB-ORDER-NUM
CCLCOM                        SORT-HYB-REC-SEQ-NUM  
CCLCOM     USING  HYB-INT-FILE  
CCLCOM     GIVING HYB-INT-FILE. 
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     MOVE ZEROES       TO WS-HYB-TBL-CNT. 
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     OPEN INPUT HYB-INT-FILE. 
CCLCOM     PERFORM 8970-READ-HYB-FILE   
CCLCOM        THRU 8970-READ-HYB-EXIT.  
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     CLOSE HYB-INT-FILE.  
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM 4000-HYB-SAIL-FILE-EXIT. 
CCLCOM     EXIT.

CCLCOM 4050-ASG-FILE.   
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     MOVE TDP01-SHIP-CODE          TO HYB-CSFA-SHIP.  
CCLCOM     MOVE TDP01-SAIL-MONTH         TO WS-F-HOLD-MOD-MONTH.
CCLCOM     MOVE TDP01-SAIL-DAY           TO WS-F-HOLD-MOD-DAYS. 
CCLCOM     MOVE TDP01-SAIL-YEAR          TO WS-F-HOLD-MOD-YEAR. 
CCLCOM     MOVE WS-F-HOLD-MOD-SAIL-DATE  TO HYB-CSFA-DATE.  
CCLCOM     MOVE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS      TO HYB-CSFA-DAYS.  
CCLCOM     MOVE TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE.
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     CALL 'ERACSF' USING HYB-CSF-ASGA-IMAGE   
CCLCOM                             HYB-CSF-STATUS   
CCLCOM     DISPLAY 'HYB-CSF-ASG-IMAGE - ' HYB-CSF-STATUS   UPON PRINTER.
CCLCOM     IF  HYB-CSF-STATUS NOT = 0   
CCLCOM         DISPLAY 'PROBLEM ASSIGNING FILE '
CCLCOM                                    HYB-CSF-ASGA2 UPON PRINTER
CCLCOM         GO TO 4050-ASG-FILE-EXIT 
CCLCOM     END-IF.  
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     MOVE HYB-CSF-FILEA-NAME     TO HYB-CSF-USE2  
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     CALL 'ERACSF' USING HYB-CSF-USE-IMAGE, HYB-CSF-STATUS.   
CCLCOM     DISPLAY HYB-CSF-USE-IMAGE '-' HYB-CSF-STATUS    UPON PRINTER.
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     IF  HYB-CSF-STATUS NOT = 0   
CCLCOM         DISPLAY 'PROBLEM ON @USE F: ' HYB-CSF-USE-IMAGE  
CCLCOM                                       UPON PRINTER   
CCLCOM         GO TO 9000-END-OF-JOB
CCLCOM     END-IF.  
CCLCOM     MOVE 'Y'      TO WS-HYB-FILE-FOUND.  
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM 4050-ASG-FILE-EXIT.  
CCLCOM     EXIT.

       4140-LOOP-SIBR1. 
           ADD 1                       TO SUB.  
           IF  SUB > SIBR-ENTRY-CTR 
               GO TO 4140-LOOP-SIBR1-EXIT   
           END-IF.  

           IF  (SIBR-BOOK-STATUS (SUB) = 'C'    )    OR 
               (SIBR-BOOK-NUM (SUB)    = SPACES )    OR 
               ( SIBR-BOOK-TYPE (SUB)  = 'U' )  
               GO TO 4140-LOOP-SIBR1
           END-IF.  

           MOVE SIBR-BOOK-NUM  (SUB)   TO WS-BOOK-NUM.  

           MOVE SIBR-BOOK-TYPE (SUB)   TO WS-BOOK-TYPE. 
           MOVE 'N'                    TO WS-ERROR-IND. 
           PERFORM 8700-CALL-RSO23 THRU 
                   8700-CALL-RSO23-EXIT.

           IF  WS-RSO23-ERROR = 'Y' 
               GO TO 4140-LOOP-SIBR1
           END-IF.

           IF  WSSL1-SAIL-DATE = RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE
               CONTINUE 
           ELSE 
               GO TO 4140-LOOP-SIBR1
           END-IF.  

CCLCOM     IF WS-HYB-FILE-FOUND = 'Y' AND RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM > SPACES

                 PERFORM 4160-SHORE-ORDERS THRU 
                         4160-SHORE-ORDERS-EXIT

CCLCOM     END-IF.

780096*    END-IF.

PPC470              EXEC SQL
PPC470                 SELECT IFNULL(ITC.SENIOR_AMT *   
PPC470                                          :TDP01-DURATION-DAYS,0) 
PPC470                       ,IFNULL(ITC.ADULT_AMT  *   
PPC470                                          :TDP01-DURATION-DAYS,0) 
PPC470                       ,IFNULL(ITC.CHILD_AMT *
PPC470                                          :TDP01-DURATION-DAYS,0) 
PPC470                       ,IFNULL(ITC.INFANT_AMT *   
PPC470                                          :TDP01-DURATION-DAYS,0) 
PPC470                   INTO :WS-SENIOR-PPG-AMT
PPC470                       ,:WS-ADULT-PPG-AMT 
PPC470                       ,:WS-CHILD-PPG-AMT 
PPC470                       ,:WS-INFANT-PPG-AMT
PPC470                 FROM  PRP_ITEM         PI
PPC470                 JOIN  ITM_PP_GRATS_CAT ITC   
PPC470                   ON  ITC.ITEM_TYPE       = PI.ITEM_TYPE 
PPC470                  AND  ITC.ITEM_CODE       =  PI.ITEM_CODE
PPC470                  AND (META_CODE           =  
PPC470                                        :TDP01-META-CODE (WS-SUB) 
PPC470                    OR META_CODE           = '~~')
PPC470                  AND (CATEGORY_CODE       =  
PPC470                                    :TDP01-CATEGORY-CODE (WS-SUB) 
PPC470                    OR CATEGORY_CODE       = '~~~')   
PPC470                WHERE  PI.PRP_OWNER_CODE   = 'SAL'
PPC470                  AND  PI.PRP_OWNER_ID     =  
PPC470                        :RSP06-PAX-SAIL-MST-OWNER-ID (WS-PAX-SUB) 
PPC470                  AND  PI.ITEM_TYPE        = 'PG' 
PPC470                  AND (PI.EMBARK_PORT_CODE IS NULL
PPC470                    OR PI.EMBARK_PORT_CODE = '   '
PPC470                    OR PI.EMBARK_PORT_CODE =  
PPC470                                         :TDP01-EMBARK-PORT-CODE) 
PPC470                  AND (PI.DEBARK_PORT_CODE IS NULL
PPC470                    OR PI.DEBARK_PORT_CODE = '   '
PPC470                    OR PI.DEBARK_PORT_CODE =  
PPC470                                         :TDP01-DEBARK-PORT-CODE) 
PPC470                  AND (PI.DURATION_DAYS IS NULL   
PPC470                    OR PI.DURATION_DAYS    = 0
PPC470                    OR PI.DURATION_DAYS    =  
PPC470                                            :TDP01-DURATION-DAYS) 
PPC470                  FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY  
PPC470              END-EXEC
PPC470              IF SQLCODE < 0  
PPC470                 GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR
PPC470              END-IF  
PPC470              IF SQLCODE = 100
PPC470                 CONTINUE 
PPC470              ELSE

PPC470              END-IF

PPC470        END-IF

198295*                WHEN 1

PPC470        ELSE  
PPC470           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
PPC470                   UNTIL WS-SUB > TDP01-SAIL-CAT-CNT OR   
PPC470                   TDP01-CATEGORY-CODE (WS-SUB) = 
PPC470                                         RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE  
PPC470              CONTINUE
PPC470           END-PERFORM
PPC470           IF WS-SUB > TDP01-SAIL-CAT-CNT 
PPC470              DISPLAY 'IBR Category not found on Sailing:'
PPC470                    RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE UPON PRINTER  
PPC470              GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR   
PPC470           END-IF 
PPC470           EXEC SQL   
PPC470              SELECT IFNULL(ITC.SENIOR_AMT *  
PPC470                                         :TDP01-DURATION-DAYS,0)  
PPC470                    ,IFNULL(ITC.ADULT_AMT  *  
PPC470                                         :TDP01-DURATION-DAYS,0)  
PPC470                    ,IFNULL(ITC.CHILD_AMT *   
PPC470                                         :TDP01-DURATION-DAYS,0)  
PPC470                    ,IFNULL(ITC.INFANT_AMT *  
PPC470                                         :TDP01-DURATION-DAYS,0)  
PPC470                INTO :WS-SENIOR-PPG-AMT   
PPC470                    ,:WS-ADULT-PPG-AMT
PPC470                    ,:WS-CHILD-PPG-AMT
PPC470                    ,:WS-INFANT-PPG-AMT   
PPC470              FROM  PRP_ITEM         PI   
PPC470              JOIN  ITM_PP_GRATS_CAT ITC  
PPC470                ON  ITC.ITEM_TYPE       = PI.ITEM_TYPE
PPC470               AND  ITC.ITEM_CODE       =  PI.ITEM_CODE   
PPC470               AND (META_CODE           = 
PPC470                                        :TDP01-META-CODE (WS-SUB) 
PPC470                 OR META_CODE           = '~~')   
PPC470               AND (CATEGORY_CODE       = 
PPC470                                    :TDP01-CATEGORY-CODE (WS-SUB) 
PPC470                 OR CATEGORY_CODE       = '~~~')  
PPC470             WHERE  PI.PRP_OWNER_CODE   = 'SAL'   
PPC470               AND  PI.PRP_OWNER_ID     = 
PPC470                        :RSP06-PAX-SAIL-MST-OWNER-ID (WS-PAX-SUB) 
PPC470               AND  PI.ITEM_TYPE        = 'PG'
PPC470               AND (PI.EMBARK_PORT_CODE IS NULL   
PPC470                 OR PI.EMBARK_PORT_CODE = '   '   
PPC470                 OR PI.EMBARK_PORT_CODE = 
PPC470                                         :TDP01-EMBARK-PORT-CODE) 
PPC470               AND (PI.DEBARK_PORT_CODE IS NULL   
PPC470                 OR PI.DEBARK_PORT_CODE = '   '   
PPC470                 OR PI.DEBARK_PORT_CODE = 
PPC470                                         :TDP01-DEBARK-PORT-CODE) 
PPC470               AND (PI.DURATION_DAYS IS NULL  
PPC470                 OR PI.DURATION_DAYS    = 0   
PPC470                 OR PI.DURATION_DAYS    = :TDP01-DURATION-DAYS)   
PPC470               FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY 
PPC470           END-EXEC   
PPC470           IF SQLCODE < 0 
PPC470              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR   
PPC470           END-IF 
PPC470           IF SQLCODE = 100   
PPC470              MOVE ZEROES        TO WSPAX-PP-GRATS-AMT
PPC470           ELSE   
PPC470              EVALUATE RSP06-PAX-SEX (WS-PAX-SUB) 
PPC470                  WHEN 'C'
PPC470                     MOVE WS-CHILD-PPG-AMT
PPC470                                 TO WSPAX-PP-GRATS-AMT
PPC470                  WHEN 'I'
PPC470                     MOVE WS-INFANT-PPG-AMT   
PPC470                                    TO WSPAX-PP-GRATS-AMT 
PPC470                  WHEN OTHER  
PPC470                     IF RSP06-PAX-AGE-CODE (WS-PAX-SUB) = 'Y' AND 
PPC470                        RSP06-PAX-AGE (WS-PAX-SUB) <  
PPC470                              TDP01-MIN-SENIOR-AGE
PPC470                        MOVE WS-ADULT-PPG-AMT 
PPC470                                    TO WSPAX-PP-GRATS-AMT 
PPC470                     ELSE 
PPC470                        MOVE WS-SENIOR-PPG-AMT
PPC470                                    TO WSPAX-PP-GRATS-AMT 
PPC470                     END-IF   
PPC470              END-EVALUATE
PPC470           END-IF 
PPC470        END-IF
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470        MOVE ZEROES              TO WSPAX-PP-GRATS-AMT
PPC470     END-IF.

                   IF  ((RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-CODE(WS-PAX-SUB PX) = ' '
                       OR 'G')  
                       AND (PAX-PPG-SW = 'N'))  
                       MOVE 'G'        TO WSPAX-COMMENT-CODE (PX)   
                       MOVE 'Y'        TO PAX-PPG-SW
                   ELSE 
                       MOVE RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-CODE (WS-PAX-SUB PX)  
                                       TO WSPAX-COMMENT-CODE  (PX)  
                   END-IF   
               END-PERFORM  
           ELSE 
               PERFORM WITH TEST BEFORE 
                   VARYING PX FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL PX > 5  
                   MOVE RSP06-PAX-COMMENT-CODE    (WS-PAX-SUB PX)   
                                       TO WSPAX-COMMENT-CODE (PX)   
               END-PERFORM  
           END-IF.  

           IF  RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-CODE (WS-PAX-SUB 1) > SPACES  
               MOVE 'N'                TO PAX-OCC-SW-1  
               PERFORM WITH TEST BEFORE 
                   VARYING PX FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL PX > 5  
                   IF  (((WSPAX-COMMENT-CODE (PX) = ' ') OR 
                         (RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-CODE (WS-PAX-SUB 1))) AND  
                        (PAX-OCC-SW-1 = 'N'))   
                       MOVE RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-CODE (WS-PAX-SUB 1)  
                                       TO WSPAX-COMMENT-CODE (PX)   
                       MOVE 'Y'        TO PAX-OCC-SW-1  
                   END-IF   
               END-PERFORM  
           END-IF.  

           IF  RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-CODE (WS-PAX-SUB 2) > SPACES  
               MOVE 'N'                    TO PAX-OCC-SW-2  
               PERFORM WITH TEST BEFORE 
                   VARYING PX FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL PX > 5  
                   IF  (((WSPAX-COMMENT-CODE (PX) = ' ') OR 
                         (RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-CODE (WS-PAX-SUB 2) ))AND  
                        (PAX-OCC-SW-2 = 'N'))   
                       MOVE RSP06-PAX-OCCASION-CODE (WS-PAX-SUB 2)  
                                       TO WSPAX-COMMENT-CODE (PX)   
                       MOVE 'Y'        TO PAX-OCC-SW-2  
                   END-IF   
               END-PERFORM  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE ZEROS                      TO PAX-DISC-PCT. 
108267*    MOVE SPACES                     TO PAX-SUBCATEGORY.  

           INITIALIZE PIAR-INFO PIDAR-INFO  
                      PIID-INFO PIER-INFO   
                      PICRC-INFO PICF-INFO  
                      PICH-INFO PICOT-INFO  
                      PICF-INFO.

218020     MOVE SPACES                 TO WSPIID-VISA-STATUS
218020                                    WSPIID-TRAVELER-NUMBER
218020                                    WSPIID-REDRESS-NUMBER.

           PERFORM 4220-MOVE-PIAR-INFO THRU 
                   4220-MOVE-PIAR-INFO-EXIT.
           PERFORM 9800-PIAR-FIX-IT THRU
                   9800-PIAR-FIX-IT-EXIT.   
           PERFORM 4260-MOVE-PICF-INFO THRU 
                   4260-MOVE-PICF-INFO-EXIT.
           PERFORM 4280-MOVE-PIID-INFO THRU 
                   4280-MOVE-PIID-INFO-EXIT 
               VARYING WS-PIID-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
               UNTIL   WS-PIID-SUB > 5  
           PERFORM 4300-MOVE-PIER-INFO THRU 
                   4300-MOVE-PIER-INFO-EXIT.
           PERFORM 4320-MOVE-PIDAR-INFO THRU
                   4320-MOVE-PIDAR-INFO-EXIT.   

           IF  RSP06-PICH-CNT (WS-PAX-SUB) > ZERO   
               PERFORM VARYING WS-PICH-CNT FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL WS-PICH-CNT > RSP06-PICH-CNT (WS-PAX-SUB)  
                   IF  RSP06-PICH-CHECK-OUT-DATE(WS-PAX-SUB WS-PICH-CNT)
PPC470*                 > SAIL1-SAIL-DATE AND   
PPC470                  >  TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD AND 
                      (RSP06-PICH-CHECK-IN-DATE(WS-PAX-SUB WS-PICH-CNT) 
160400                  > ZEROES) AND   
160400                (RSP06-PICH-IROPS-CNT(WS-PAX-SUB WS-PICH-CNT) = 0)
160400                    PERFORM 4330-MOVE-PICH-INFO THRU  
160400                            4330-MOVE-PICH-INFO-EXIT  
160400*                   MOVE RSP06-PICH-ENTRY(WS-PAX-SUB WS-PICH-CNT) 
160400*                                   TO PICH-INFO  
                          MOVE RSP06-PICH-CNT(WS-PAX-SUB)   
                                          TO WS-PICH-CNT
                   END-IF   
              END-PERFORM   
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM 8850-CALL-PAX-FEE THRU   
                   8850-CALL-PAX-FEE-EXIT.  

           MOVE 'PAX '                 TO BBR-TYPE. 
           MOVE WSPAX-SHIP-CODE        TO BBR-SHIP. 
PPC470*    MOVE SAIL1-SAIL-DATE        TO BBR-DATE. 
PPC470     MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE    TO BBR-DATE.

                                       TO NR-NATIONALITY-CODE.

               WSPAX-SEQ-NUM    = ZEROES
               MOVE SEQ-HIGH           TO WSPAX-SEQ-NUM 
               ADD 1                   TO SEQ-HIGH  
           END-IF.

      ******************************************************************
      *TO FETCH NATIONALITY-REC FOR A PAX                              *
      ******************************************************************

       4360-FETCH-NATIONALITY.  

           MOVE 'PAX NATLTY FETCH    ' TO PROBLEM-FETCH.

           FETCH NATIONALITY-REC RECORD 
               SUPPRESS ALL CURRENCY UPDATES
               ON ERROR GO TO 4360-FETCH-NATIONALITY-EXIT.  

       4360-FETCH-NATIONALITY-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

       4361-MAKE-NOTE.  

           IF  ERROR-NUM > 0000 
               MOVE RSP06-PAX-BOOK-NUM (WS-PAX-SUB) 
                                       TO MAPPER-BKG
               MOVE RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM (WS-PAX-SUB)  
                                       TO MAPPER-SEQ
               MOVE RSP06-PAX-NATIONALITY-CODE (WS-PAX-SUB) 
                                       TO MAPPER-NAT
               DISPLAY MAPPER-MESSAGE1 UPON PRINTER 
               WRITE RUN-REC FROM MAPPER-MESSAGE1   
           ELSE 
               PERFORM 4362-NR-TABLE THRU   
                       4362-NR-TABLE-EXIT   
           END-IF.  

       4361-MAKE-NOTE-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

       4362-NR-TABLE.   

           SET NR-IDX                  TO 1.

           SEARCH NR-CODE VARYING NR-IDX
               AT END GO TO 4363-SAVE-NR
               WHEN NR-CODE (NR-IDX) =  
                    RSP06-PAX-NATIONALITY-CODE (WS-PAX-SUB) 
                    GO TO 4362-NR-TABLE-EXIT.   

       4363-SAVE-NR.

           MOVE RSP06-PAX-NATIONALITY-CODE(WS-PAX-SUB)  
                                       TO NR-CODE (NR-TABLE-SIZE).  
           ADD 1                       TO NR-TABLE-SIZE.

       4362-NR-TABLE-EXIT.  
           EXIT.

       4340-GET-CIF-INFO.   

           MOVE ZEROES                 TO WS-RCF-COUNT. 
           MOVE SPACES                 TO RCF-KEY.  
PPC470*    MOVE RSP06-PAX-PRICE-TYPE(WS-PAX-SUB)
PPC470*                                TO RCF-PAX-PRICE-TYPE.   
PPC470*    MOVE RSP06-PAX-PRICE-CODE(WS-PAX-SUB)
PPC470*                                TO RCF-PAX-PRICE-CODE.   

           READ RATE-CARD-FILE  
               INVALID KEY GO TO 4341-NOT-A-FREEBEE.

           MOVE 1                      TO WS-RCF-COUNT. 


       4341-NOT-A-FREEBEE.  


           IF  RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM(WS-PAX-SUB) < '0000000000' OR   
               RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM(WS-PAX-SUB) > '9999999999' OR   
               RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM(WS-PAX-SUB) NOT NUMERIC 
               GO TO 4342-GET-CIF-INFO-NO-FIND  
           END-IF.  

           IF  RSP06-PAX-PAST-IND(WS-PAX-SUB) NOT   = 'Y'          AND  
               RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM(WS-PAX-SUB) > '0000000000'  
               MOVE 'Y'                TO WSPAX-PAST-IND
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'CIF'                  TO CIF-CALCAREA. 
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM (WS-PAX-SUB) (10:1)
                                       TO CIF-CALCAREA (4:1).   
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM (WS-PAX-SUB)   
                                       TO CIF-CUSTOMER-ID.  

           FETCH CIF-REC RECORD 
               ON ERROR GO TO 4342-GET-CIF-INFO-NO-FIND.

           MOVE RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM(WS-PAX-SUB)
                                       TO CIF-CUSTOMER-ID.  

           FETCH CIF-REC RECORD 
               ON ERROR GO TO 4342-GET-CIF-INFO-NO-FIND.

           IF  CIF-CRUISE-CNT > 0   
               IF  WS-RCF-COUNT = ZEROES
                   MOVE CIF-CRUISE-CNT TO WSCIF-CRUISE-CNT  
               ELSE 
                   MOVE 1              TO WSCIF-CRUISE-CNT  
               END-IF   
           ELSE 
               MOVE 1                  TO WSCIF-CRUISE-CNT  
           END-IF.  

113068     MOVE CIF-TIER-CODE          TO WSCIF-TIER-CODE.  
113068     MOVE RSP06-PDI-SAIL-TIER-CODE(WS-PAX-SUB)
113068                                 TO  WSPAX-TREAT-TIER.

191395     IF  WS-COLOUR-FLAG = 'Y' 
191395         STRING WSPAX-BOOK-NUM, WSPAX-SEQ-NUM 
191395                DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO SCIF-INX-KEY   
191395         READ SS-COLOUR-INX-FILE  
191395              INVALID KEY 
191395                 PERFORM 4341-WRITE-ERR-FILE THRU 
191395                         4341-WRITE-ERR-FILE-EXIT 
191395                 GO TO 4341-DECIDE-TREAT-TIER 
191395         END-READ 
191395         MOVE SCIF-SS-COLOUR-NUM     TO WSPAX-TREAT-TIER  
191395     END-IF.  

191395 4341-DECIDE-TREAT-TIER.  

113068     IF  CIF-TIER-CODE NOT = SPACES   
113068         IF  CIF-TIER-CODE NOT = WSPAX-TREAT-TIER 
113068             MOVE 'Y'            TO WS-MOVED-TIER 
113068         ELSE 
113068             MOVE 'N'            TO WS-MOVED-TIER 
113068         END-IF   
113068     ELSE 
113068         MOVE 'N'                TO WS-MOVED-TIER 
113068     END-IF.  

           GO TO 4340-GET-CIF-INFO-EXIT.

191395 4341-WRITE-ERR-FILE. 

191395     SET WS-BKG-INX-KEY          TO 1.
191395     SEARCH WS-BKG-REC
191395        AT END
191395           GO TO 4341-WRITE-ERR-FILE-EXIT 
191395        WHEN WS-BOOK1-NUM (WS-BKG-INX-KEY) = WSPAX-BOOK-NUM   
191395           NEXT SENTENCE. 

191395     INITIALIZE REPORT-REC.   
191395     MOVE ','                    TO RF-FILLER1 RF-FILLER2 
191395                                    RF-FILLER3 RF-FILLER4 
191395                                    RF-FILLER5 RF-FILLER6 
191395                                    RF-FILLER7.   
191395     MOVE WSPAX-BOOK-NUM         TO RF-PAX-BOOK-NUM.  
191395     MOVE WSPAX-CABIN-NUM        TO RF-PAX-CABIN-NUM. 
191395     MOVE WSPAX-SEQ-NUM          TO RF-PAX-SEQ-NUM.   
191395     MOVE WSPAX-CABIN-SEQ-NUM    TO RF-PAX-CABIN-SEQ-NUM. 
191395     MOVE WSPAX-CABIN-BOOK-NUM   TO RF-PAX-CABIN-BOOK-NUM.
191395     MOVE WSPAX-SHIP-CODE        TO RF-PAX-SHIP-CODE. 
191395     MOVE WSPAX-SAIL-DATE        TO RF-PAX-SAIL-DATE. 

191395     WRITE REPORT-REC.

191395 4341-WRITE-ERR-FILE-EXIT.
191395     EXIT.

       4342-GET-CIF-INFO-NO-FIND.   

           IF  WSPAX-PAST-IND = 'Y' 
               MOVE 1                  TO WSCIF-CRUISE-CNT  
           ELSE 
               MOVE 0                  TO WSCIF-CRUISE-CNT  
           END-IF.  
113068     MOVE SPACES                 TO WSCIF-TIER-CODE.  
113068     MOVE RSP06-PDI-SAIL-TIER-CODE(WS-PAX-SUB)
113068                                 TO  WSPAX-TREAT-TIER.
113068     MOVE 'Y'                    TO WS-MOVED-TIER .   

191395     IF  WS-COLOUR-FLAG = 'Y' 
191395         STRING WSPAX-BOOK-NUM, WSPAX-SEQ-NUM 
191395                DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO SCIF-INX-KEY   
191395         READ SS-COLOUR-INX-FILE  
191395              INVALID KEY 
191395                 PERFORM 4341-WRITE-ERR-FILE THRU 
191395                         4341-WRITE-ERR-FILE-EXIT 
191395                 GO TO 4340-GET-CIF-INFO-EXIT 
191395         END-READ 
191395         MOVE SCIF-SS-COLOUR-NUM     TO WSPAX-TREAT-TIER  
191395     END-IF.  

           GO TO 4340-GET-CIF-INFO-EXIT.

       4340-GET-CIF-INFO-EXIT.

       4424-I-AGENT-TABLE.  

           SET I-AT-IDX                TO 1.

           SEARCH I-AT-CUSTOMER-NUM VARYING I-AT-IDX
               AT END GO TO 4426-SAVE-I-AGENT   
               WHEN I-AT-CUSTOMER-NUM (I-AT-IDX) =  
                    RSP06-IBR-CUSTOMER-NUM  
                   GO TO 4424-I-AGENT-TABLE-EXIT.   

       4426-SAVE-I-AGENT.   

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-CUSTOMER-NUM TO I-AT-CUSTOMER-NUM 
                                                  (I-AGENT-TABLE-SIZE). 
           ADD 1                       TO I-AGENT-TABLE-SIZE.   

       4424-I-AGENT-TABLE-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

CCLCOM*           END-IF
CCLCOM*        ELSE 
CCLCOM*           WRITE BIG-CONT-BUCKET-REC 
CCLCOM*           ADD 1                TO WS-SHOREX-RECS-WRITTEN
CCLCOM*                                   CONT-COUNT
CCLCOM*        END-IF   
CCLCOM*    END-IF.

CCLCOM*     END-PERFORM.
CCLCOM* 
CCLCOM*4166-VALIDATE-EGOR-EXIT. 
CCLCOM*     EXIT.

       5326-GBR-GET-SPNR.   

           MOVE 0                      TO WS-FEE.

PPC470*              WS-FEE-FOUND

PPC470*        END-IF

PPC470     EXEC SQL 
PPC470        SELECT FEE_CODE   
PPC470              ,PHASE_NUM  
PPC470          INTO :WS-FEE-CODE   
PPC470              ,:WS-FEE-PHASE-NUM  
PPC470          FROM SAIL_FEE   
PPC470         WHERE SHIP_CODE  = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE  
PPC470           AND SAIL_ID    = :TDP01-SAIL-ID
PPC470           AND FEE_CODE IN ('INTD', 'INTC')   
PPC470        FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
PPC470     END-EXEC.

PPC470     IF SQLCODE < 0   
PPC470        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470        IF SQLCODE = 100  
PPC470           MOVE 0 TO WS-FEE   
PPC470        ELSE  
PPC470           MOVE 1 TO WS-FEE   
PPC470        END-IF
PPC470     END-IF.  

           IF  WS-FEE-FOUND 
               COMPUTE WS-FEE-SUB = WS-FEE-SUB - 1

144449*            MOVE RSP27-O-FEE-AMT

PPC470           AND SAL.SAIL_ID           = :TDP01-SAIL-ID

PPC470                                    WSGBR-ADMIN-FEE-AMT   
PPC470*        COMPUTE WSGBR-PTCHG-RATE   = ( MRR-PTCHG-AMT 
PPC470*                                   *   RSP74-PRC-XCHG-RATE ) 
PPC470*                                   /   RSP74-GB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE
PPC470         COMPUTE WSGBR-PTCHG-RATE   = ( TDP01-NON-COMM-AMT
PPC470                                    *   RSP74-PRC-XCHG-RATE ) 
PPC470                                    /   RSP74-GB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE
PPC470*        COMPUTE WSGBR-ADMIN-FEE-AMT = ( MRR-ADMIN-FEE-AMT
PPC470*                                    *   RSP74-PRC-XCHG-RATE )
PPC470*                                    /  RSP74-GB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE
PPC470*        COMPUTE WSGBR-DAMAGE-DEP-AMT = ( MRR-DAMAGE-DEPOSIT-AMT  
PPC470*                                     *   RSP74-PRC-XCHG-RATE )   
PPC470*                                     / RSP74-GB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE
PPC470*    END-IF.  

PPC470     EXEC SQL 
PPC470        SELECT SDD.DAMAGE_DEPOSIT_AMT 
PPC470          INTO :WSGBR-DAMAGE-DEP-AMT  
PPC470          FROM SAILING              SAL   
PPC470          JOIN SAIL_DAMAGE_DEPOSIT  SDD   
PPC470            ON SDD.SHIP_CODE      = SAL.SHIP_CODE 
PPC470           AND SDD.SAIL_ID        = SAL.SAIL_ID   
PPC470           AND SDD.PRP_OWNER_CODE = SAL.PRP_OWNER_CODE
PPC470           AND SDD.PRP_OWNER_ID   = SAL.PRP_OWNER_ID  
PPC470           AND (SDD.CURRENCY_CODE IS NULL 
PPC470             OR SDD.CURRENCY_CODE = :GBR1-CURRENCY-CODE)  
PPC470         WHERE SAL.SHIP_CODE      = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE  
PPC470           AND SAL.SAIL_ID        = :TDP01-SAIL-ID
PPC470       FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY 
PPC470     END-EXEC.

PPC470     IF SQLCODE < 0   
PPC470        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470        IF SQLCODE = 100  
PPC470           MOVE ZEROES TO WSGBR-DAMAGE-DEP-AMT
PPC470        END-IF
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     COMPUTE WSGBR-DAMAGE-DEP-AMT = ( WSGBR-DAMAGE-DEP-AMT
PPC470                                  *   RSP74-PRC-XCHG-RATE )   
PPC470                                  / RSP74-GB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE.

                   MOVE WS-COMP-GRATS  TO WSGBR-TOTAL-PP-GRATS-AMT

                   6400-AVAIL-WRITE-EXIT.   

           MOVE 'AVAIL-CAB1-FETCH 2  ' TO PROBLEM-FETCH.
           FETCH NEXT RECORD WITHIN 
               AVAIL-OCC-CABIN-SET SET  
               AT END GO TO 6300-AVAIL-CAB-EXIT.

           GO TO 6300-AVAIL-CAB.

       6300-AVAIL-CAB-EXIT. 
           EXIT.


       6400-AVAIL-WRITE.

           MOVE AVAIL-SHIP-CODE        TO BBR-SHIP. 
           MOVE AVAIL-SAIL-DATE        TO BBR-DATE.

               VARYING BBR-CHK FROM 1 BY 1

               MOVE ITR-CONF-DESC(SUB) TO WSITR-CONF-DESC (SUB)

           END-IF.

       7100-GROUP-AGENT.

           IF  G-AT-CUSTOMER-NUM (SUB) = SPACES OR ZEROES   
               GO TO 7100-GROUP-AGENT-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE SPACES                 TO AAR-CUSTOMER-KEY. 
           MOVE 'CM'                   TO AAR-CUSTOMER-TYPE.
           MOVE G-AT-CUSTOMER-NUM(SUB) TO AAR-CUSTOMER-NUM. 
           MOVE 'AAR-FETCH           ' TO PROBLEM-FETCH.
           FETCH AGENCY-ASSOCIATION-REC RECORD  
               ON ERROR GO TO 7100-GROUP-AGENT-EXIT.

           MOVE 'AGENT-FETCH         '      TO PROBLEM-FETCH.   
           FETCH OWNER WITHIN AGENCY-ASSOCIATION-SET SET
               ON ERROR GO TO 7100-GROUP-AGENT-EXIT.

           PERFORM 7800-AGENT-MOVE-WRITE THRU   
                   7800-AGENT-MOVE-WRITE-EXIT.  

       7100-GROUP-AGENT-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

                       RSP23-I-INDV-BOOK-NUM UPON PRINTER   
               MOVE 'Y'                TO WS-RSO23-ERROR
186097         GO TO 9500-RETURN-SUBROUTINE-ERROR   
           END-IF.  

       8700-CALL-RSO23-EXIT.
           EXIT.

       8750-CALL-RSO74-PARA.

           INITIALIZE RSP74-GBR-OUTPUT. 
           MOVE 'Y'                    TO RSP74-I-READ-GBR-IND  
                                          RSP74-I-READ-GFR-IND. 
           MOVE 'B'                    TO RSP74-I-CONV-RATE-BASE.   
           MOVE 'N'                    TO WS-RSO74-ERROR.   

           CALL 'RSO74GBR'    USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS
                                    DPP01-COMMON-INFO   
                                    RSP74-GBR-INPUT 
                                    RSP74-GBR-OUTPUT.   

144449*    PERFORM DPP12-ERROR-CHECK.   

           IF  DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR        > 0 AND 
               DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 0 
               DISPLAY DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) ',' 
                       DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1)  ',' 
                       DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) ', GROUP BK# =' 
                       RSP74-I-GBR-BOOK-NUM                UPON PRINTER 
               MOVE 'Y'                TO WS-RSO74-ERROR
186097         GO TO 9500-RETURN-SUBROUTINE-ERROR   
           END-IF.  

       8750-CALL-RSO74-PARA-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       8800-CALL-BOOKING-FEE.   

           MOVE ZEROES                 TO WSIBR-INTL-DEP-AMT
                                          WSIBR-USTTA-TAX-AMT   
                                          WSIBR-CUSTOMS-FEE-AMT 
                                          WSIBR-IMMIGRATION-TAX-AMT 
                                          WSIBR-CRUISE-FUEL-AMT 
                                          WSIBR-CITY-FUEL-AMT   
                                          WSIBR-CITY-FACILITY-AMT   
                                          WS-IBR-CRUISE-SUPP-FEE-AMT
                                          WS-IBR-SECURITY-FEE-AMT   
190487                                    WSIBR-APHI-SERVICE-FEE.   

           PERFORM VARYING WS-FEE-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
               UNTIL WS-FEE-SUB > RSP06-IFR-ENTRY-CTR   
               EVALUATE RSP06-IFR-FEE-CODE           (WS-FEE-SUB)   
                   WHEN 'INTD'  
                        ADD RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT  (WS-FEE-SUB)   
                                       TO WSIBR-INTL-DEP-AMT
                   WHEN 'INTC'  
                        ADD  RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (WS-FEE-SUB)   
                                       TO WSIBR-INTL-DEP-AMT
                   WHEN 'INTF'  
                        ADD  RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (WS-FEE-SUB)   
                                       TO WSIBR-INTL-DEP-AMT
                   WHEN 'USTT'  
                        ADD RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT  (WS-FEE-SUB)   
                                       TO WSIBR-USTTA-TAX-AMT   
                   WHEN 'CSTM'  
                        ADD RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT  (WS-FEE-SUB)   
                                       TO WSIBR-CUSTOMS-FEE-AMT 
                   WHEN 'IMGT'  
                        ADD RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT  (WS-FEE-SUB)   
                                       TO WSIBR-IMMIGRATION-TAX-AMT 
                   WHEN 'CRFU'  
                        ADD RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT  (WS-FEE-SUB)   
                                       TO WSIBR-CRUISE-FUEL-AMT 
                   WHEN 'CTFU'  
                        ADD RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT  (WS-FEE-SUB)   
                                       TO WSIBR-CITY-FUEL-AMT   
                   WHEN 'CTSE'  
                        ADD RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT  (WS-FEE-SUB)   
                                       TO WS-IBR-SECURITY-FEE-AMT   
                   WHEN 'CFUL'  
                        ADD RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT  (WS-FEE-SUB)   
                                       TO WS-IBR-CRUISE-SUPP-FEE-AMT
                   WHEN 'CFU2'  
                        ADD RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT  (WS-FEE-SUB)   
                                       TO WS-IBR-CRUISE-SUPP-FEE-AMT
                   WHEN 'CTFA'  
                        ADD RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (WS-FEE-SUB)
                                       TO WSIBR-CITY-FACILITY-AMT   
190487             WHEN 'APHI'  
190487                  ADD RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (WS-FEE-SUB)
190487                                 TO WSIBR-APHI-SERVICE-FEE
               END-EVALUATE 
           END-PERFORM. 

       8800-CALL-BOOKING-FEE-EXIT.  
            EXIT.   

       8850-CALL-PAX-FEE.   

           MOVE ZEROES                 TO PAX-INTL-DEP-AMT  
                                          PAX-USTTA-TAX-AMT 
                                          PAX-CUSTOMS-FEE-AMT   
                                          PAX-IMMIGRATION-TAX-AMT   
                                          PAX-CRUISE-FUEL-AMT   
                                          PAX-CITY-FUEL-AMT 
                                          PAX-CITY-FACILITY-AMT 
                                          WS-PAX-CRUISE-SUPP-FEE-AMT
                                          WS-PAX-SECURITY-FEE-AMT   
190487                                    WSPAX-APHI-SERVICE-FEE.   

           PERFORM VARYING WS-FEE-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
               UNTIL WS-FEE-SUB > RSP06-PXFR-CNT (WS-PAX-SUB)   
               EVALUATE RSP06-PXFR-FEE-CODE      (WS-PAX-SUB WS-FEE-SUB)
                   WHEN 'INTD'  
                     ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT      (WS-PAX-SUB WS-FEE-SUB)
                                       TO PAX-INTL-DEP-AMT  
                   WHEN 'INTC'  
                     ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT      (WS-PAX-SUB WS-FEE-SUB)
                                       TO PAX-INTL-DEP-AMT  
                   WHEN 'INTF'  
                     ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT      (WS-PAX-SUB WS-FEE-SUB)
                                       TO PAX-INTL-DEP-AMT  
                   WHEN 'USTT'  
                     ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT      (WS-PAX-SUB WS-FEE-SUB)
                                       TO PAX-USTTA-TAX-AMT 
                   WHEN 'CTFU'  
                     ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT      (WS-PAX-SUB WS-FEE-SUB)
                                       TO PAX-CITY-FUEL-AMT 
                   WHEN 'CTFA'  
                     ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT      (WS-PAX-SUB WS-FEE-SUB)
                                       TO PAX-CITY-FACILITY-AMT 
                   WHEN 'CTSE'  
                     ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT      (WS-PAX-SUB WS-FEE-SUB)
                                       TO WS-PAX-SECURITY-FEE-AMT   
                   WHEN 'CSTM'  
                     ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT      (WS-PAX-SUB WS-FEE-SUB)
                                       TO PAX-CUSTOMS-FEE-AMT   
                   WHEN 'CFUL'  
                     ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT      (WS-PAX-SUB WS-FEE-SUB)
                                       TO WS-PAX-CRUISE-SUPP-FEE-AMT
                   WHEN 'CFU2'  
                     ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT      (WS-PAX-SUB WS-FEE-SUB)
                                       TO WS-PAX-CRUISE-SUPP-FEE-AMT
                   WHEN 'IMGT'  
                     ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT      (WS-PAX-SUB WS-FEE-SUB)
                                       TO PAX-IMMIGRATION-TAX-AMT   
                   WHEN 'CRFU'  
                     ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT      (WS-PAX-SUB WS-FEE-SUB)
                                       TO PAX-CRUISE-FUEL-AMT   
190487             WHEN 'APHI'  
190487               ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT      (WS-PAX-SUB WS-FEE-SUB)
190487                                 TO WSPAX-APHI-SERVICE-FEE
               END-EVALUATE 
           END-PERFORM. 

       8850-CALL-PAX-FEE-EXIT.  
           EXIT.

PPC470*8900-FETCH-PMR.  

PPC470*    IF  WS-PAX-PRICE-TYPE      (T-PAX-IDX) = PMR1-PRICE-TYPE AND 
PPC470*        WS-PAX-PRICE-CODE      (T-PAX-IDX) = PMR1-PRICE-CODE AND 
PPC470*        WS-PAX-PRICE-PHASE-NUM (T-PAX-IDX) = PMR1-PHASE-NUM  
PPC470*        GO TO 8900-SET-PMR   
PPC470*    END-IF.  

169013*    IF  WS-BOOK-TYPE-FLG = 'I'   
169013*        MOVE RSP06-IBR-COMPANY-CODE  
169013*                                TO PMR1-COMPANY-CODE 
169013*    ELSE 
169013*        MOVE GBR1-COMPANY-CODE  TO PMR1-COMPANY-CODE 
169013*    END-IF.  
169013* 
169013*    MOVE WS-PAX-PRICE-CODE (T-PAX-IDX)   
169013*                                TO PMR1-PRICE-CODE.  
169013*    MOVE WS-PAX-PRICE-TYPE (T-PAX-IDX)   
169013*                                TO PMR1-PRICE-TYPE.  
169013*    MOVE WS-PAX-PRICE-PHASE-NUM  (T-PAX-IDX) 
169013*                                TO PMR1-PHASE-NUM.   
169013*    MOVE 10130925               TO DPP01-ERROR-NUMBER.   

PPC470*COPY SSP22INT.   
PPC470* 
PPC470*    IF WS-BOOK-TYPE-FLG = 'I'
PPC470*       MOVE RSP06-IBR-COMPANY-CODE      TO SSP22-I-COMPANY-CODE  
PPC470*    ELSE 
PPC470*       MOVE GBR1-COMPANY-CODE           TO SSP22-I-COMPANY-CODE  
PPC470*    END-IF.  
PPC470* 
PPC470*    MOVE WS-PAX-PRICE-TYPE (T-PAX-IDX)  TO SSP22-I-PRICE-TYPE.   
PPC470*    MOVE WS-PAX-PRICE-CODE (T-PAX-IDX)  TO SSP22-I-PRICE-CODE.   
PPC470*    MOVE WS-PAX-PRICE-PHASE-NUM  (T-PAX-IDX) 
PPC470*                                        TO SSP22-I-PHASE-NUM.
PPC470* 
PPC470*    CALL 'SSC22GPD' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS   
PPC470*                          DPP01-COMMON-INFO  
PPC470*                          SSP22-INPUT-PARAMS 
PPC470*                          SSP22-OUTPUT-PARAMS.   
PPC470* 
PPC470*    IF DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > 0 AND DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS (1) > 1
PPC470*       GO TO 9500-RETURN-SUBROUTINE-ERROR.   

PPC470*    MOVE SSP22-O-SLOT-NUM TO PAGE-NUM OF PRICING-AREA-KEY.   
PPC470*    MOVE 1                TO RECORD-NUM OF PRICING-AREA-KEY. 
PPC470* 
PPC470*    FETCH PRICING-MASTER-REC RECORD  
PPC470*        ON ERROR GO TO 8900-PMR-FETCH-ERROR .

PPC470*    IF  WS-PAX-PRICE-CODE (T-PAX-IDX)      = PMR1-PRICE-CODE AND 
PPC470*        WS-PAX-PRICE-TYPE (T-PAX-IDX)      = PMR1-PRICE-TYPE AND 
PPC470*        WS-PAX-PRICE-PHASE-NUM (T-PAX-IDX) = PMR1-PHASE-NUM  
PPC470*        GO TO 8900-SET-PMR   
PPC470*    ELSE 
PPC470*        GO TO 8900-PMR-FETCH-ERROR   
PPC470*    END-IF.  

PPC470*8900-PMR-FETCH-ERROR.
PPC470* 
PPC470*    MOVE SPACES                 TO PMR1-COMPANY-CODE 
PPC470*                                   PMR1-PRICE-TYPE   
PPC470*                                   PMR1-PRICE-CODE   
PPC470*                                   WS-OBC-MARKETING-CODE 
PPC470*                                   PMR1-CURRENCY-CODE.   
PPC470*    MOVE ZERO                   TO PMR1-PHASE-NUM.   
PPC470* 
PPC470*    GO TO 8900-FETCH-PMR-EXIT.   
PPC470* 
PPC470*8900-SET-PMR.
PPC470* 
PPC470*    MOVE PMR1-OBC-DEPT          TO WS-OBC-MARKETING-CODE.
PPC470* 
PPC470*8900-FETCH-PMR-EXIT. 
PPC470*     EXIT.   

       8950-GROUP-BOOKING-FEE.  

           MOVE ZEROES                 TO  WSGBR-TOTAL-INTL-DEP-AMT 
                                           WSGBR-TOTAL-CITY-FUEL-AMT
                                           WSGBR-TOTAL-CITY-FACILITY-AMT
                                           WSGBR-TOTAL-CRUISE-FUEL-AMT  
                                           WSGBR-TOTAL-USTTA-TAX-AMT
                                           WSGBR-TOTAL-CUSTOMS-FEE-AMT  
                                           WSGBR-TOTAL-IMMI-TAX-AMT 
190487                                     WSGBR-TOTAL-APHI-SERV-FEE.   

           PERFORM VARYING GSUB1 FROM 1 BY 1
               UNTIL GSUB1 > RSP74-GFR-ENTRY-CTR
               IF  RSP74-GFR-FEE-CODE (GSUB1) = 'INTD' OR   
                                                'INTC' OR   
                                                'INTF'  
                   ADD RSP74-GFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (GSUB1)  
                                       TO WSGBR-TOTAL-INTL-DEP-AMT  
               END-IF   
               IF  RSP74-GFR-FEE-CODE (GSUB1) = 'CTFU'  
                   MOVE RSP74-GFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (GSUB1) 
                                       TO WSGBR-TOTAL-CITY-FUEL-AMT 
               END-IF   
               IF  RSP74-GFR-FEE-CODE (GSUB1) = 'CTFA'  
                   MOVE RSP74-GFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (GSUB1) 
                                       TO WSGBR-TOTAL-CITY-FACILITY-AMT 
               END-IF   
               IF  RSP74-GFR-FEE-CODE (GSUB1) = 'CRFU'  
                   MOVE RSP74-GFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (GSUB1) 
                                       TO WSGBR-TOTAL-CRUISE-FUEL-AMT   
               END-IF   
               IF  RSP74-GFR-FEE-CODE (GSUB1) = 'USTT'  
                   MOVE RSP74-GFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (GSUB1) 
                                       TO WSGBR-TOTAL-USTTA-TAX-AMT 
               END-IF   
               IF  RSP74-GFR-FEE-CODE (GSUB1) = 'CSTM'  
                   MOVE RSP74-GFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (GSUB1) 
                                       TO WSGBR-TOTAL-CUSTOMS-FEE-AMT   
               END-IF   
               IF  RSP74-GFR-FEE-CODE (GSUB1) = 'IMGT'  
                   MOVE RSP74-GFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (GSUB1) 
                                       TO WSGBR-TOTAL-IMMI-TAX-AMT  
               END-IF   
190487         IF  RSP74-GFR-FEE-CODE (GSUB1) = 'APHI'  
190487             MOVE RSP74-GFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (GSUB1) 
190487                                 TO WSGBR-TOTAL-APHI-SERV-FEE 
190487         END-IF   
           END-PERFORM. 

       8950-GROUP-BOOKING-FEE-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

CCLCOM*        AT END PERFORM 8972-ADD-TO-HYB-BOOK-TBL  
CCLCOM*                 THRU 8972-HYB-BOOK-TBL-EXIT 
CCLCOM        WHEN WS-HYB-BOOK-NUM (WS-H-BOOK-IDX) = WS-HYB-I-BOOK-NUM  
CCLCOM             GO TO 8970-READ-HYB-FILE 
CCLCOM        WHEN WS-HYB-BOOK-NUM (WS-H-BOOK-IDX) NOT > SPACES 
CCLCOM             NEXT SENTENCE.   
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     GO TO 8972-ADD-TO-HYB-BOOK-TBL.  
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM 8970-READ-HYB-EXIT.  
CCLCOM     EXIT.

CCLCOM 8972-ADD-TO-HYB-BOOK-TBL.
CCLCOM

CCLCOM        DISPLAY 'BOOKING COUNT EXCEEDED FOR :'

CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     SET WS-H-BOOK-IDX        TO WS-HYB-BOOK-CNT. 
CCLCOM     MOVE WS-HYB-I-BOOK-NUM   TO WS-HYB-BOOK-NUM (WS-H-BOOK-IDX). 
CCLCOM     MOVE WS-HYB-TBL-CNT      TO WS-HYB-BOOK-POSITION 
CCLCOM                                 (WS-H-BOOK-IDX). 
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     GO TO 8970-READ-HYB-FILE.

CCLCOM 8980-GET-HYB-TBL-INDX.   
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     MOVE 'N'          TO WS-H-BOOK-FND.  
CCLCOM     SET WS-H-BOOK-IDX TO 1.  
CCLCOM     SEARCH WS-HYB-BOOK-TBL   
CCLCOM        AT END MOVE 'N' TO WS-H-BOOK-FND  
CCLCOM*       AT END GO TO 8980-HYB-INDX-EXIT   
CCLCOM        WHEN WS-HYB-BOOK-NUM (WS-H-BOOK-IDX) = RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM 
CCLCOM             MOVE WS-HYB-BOOK-POSITION (WS-H-BOOK-IDX)
CCLCOM                              TO WS-HYB-SUB   
CCLCOM             MOVE 'Y'         TO WS-H-BOOK-FND
CCLCOM        WHEN WS-HYB-BOOK-NUM (WS-H-BOOK-IDX) NOT > SPACES 
CCLCOM             GO TO 8980-HYB-INDX-EXIT 
CCLCOM     END-SEARCH.  
CCLCOM

CCLCOM     EXIT.

CCLCOM 8990-GET-PMT-DTL.
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     EXEC SQL 
CCLCOM          SELECT IFNULL(CC_PAYMENT_TYPE, ' ') 
CCLCOM                ,IFNULL(CC_NUM_LAST4, ' ')
CCLCOM                ,IFNULL(CC_EXP_MMYY,0)
CCLCOM           INTO :WS-PAYMENT-TYPE  
CCLCOM               ,:WS-CC-LAST4  
CCLCOM               ,:WS-CC-EXP-MMYY   
CCLCOM           FROM ORDER_PAYMENT_DETAIL  
CCLCOM          WHERE MF_ORDER_NUM = :WS-ORDER-NUM  
CCLCOM           FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY 
CCLCOM     END-EXEC.
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     IF SQLCODE < 0   
CCLCOM        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
CCLCOM     END-IF.  
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     IF SQLCODE = 100 
CCLCOM        DISPLAY 'ORDER NOT FOUND IN ORDER PAYMENT TABLE: '
CCLCOM                 WS-ORDER-NUM UPON PRINTER
CCLCOM     END-IF.  
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM 8990-PMT-DTL-EXIT.   
CCLCOM     EXIT.

CCLCOM 8991-GET-EXC-ID. 
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     EXEC SQL 
CCLCOM          SELECT EXC_ID   
CCLCOM           INTO :WS-EXC-ID
CCLCOM           FROM SMF_EXC_MASTER
CCLCOM          WHERE VENDOR_REF_NUM = :WS-HYB-T-ITEM-NUM (WS-H)
CCLCOM           FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY 
CCLCOM     END-EXEC.
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     IF SQLCODE < 0   
CCLCOM        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
CCLCOM     END-IF.  
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM     IF SQLCODE = 100 
CCLCOM        DISPLAY 'EXC ID NOT FOUND IN EXC MASTER TBL FOR REF NUM: '
CCLCOM                 WS-HYB-T-ITEM-NUM (WS-H) UPON PRINTER
CCLCOM     END-IF.  
CCLCOM  
CCLCOM 8991-EXC-ID-EXIT.
CCLCOM     EXIT.

                                     WS-SAVE-BF-FILE-NAME (WS-TOTAL-BF).
           MOVE WS-EXT-CYCLE           TO   
                                         WS-SAVE-BF-CYCLE (WS-TOTAL-BF).

           CALL 'ERACSF' USING CSF-FREE-IMAGE   
                               CSF-STATUS.  

           DISPLAY CSF-FREE-IMAGE '-' CSF-STATUS UPON PRINTER.  

           IF  CSF-STATUS NOT = 0   
               DISPLAY 'FREE FAILURE IN RB ROUTINE ' CSF-FILE-NAME  
                                                 UPON PRINTER   
            END-IF. 

       9400-CLOSE-BIG-BUCKET-EXIT.  
           EXIT.

       9700-FIX-IT. 
142635*******  ADDED LOGIC HERE TO ALLOW NEGATIVE SIGN IN FILE FOR  
142635*******  EOR-ORDER-NET-DUE FIELD, WHICH NOW CAN HAVE A NON-ZERO   
142635*******  VALUE WHICH WILL BE NEGATIVE.
142635     IF BBR-CHK = 191 AND BBR-BYTE (191) = '-'
142635        IF BIG-BUCKET-REC (1:3) = 'EOR'   
142635           GO TO 9700-FIX-IT-EXIT 
142635        END-IF
142635     END-IF.  
142635  
145563     IF  BIG-BUCKET-REC (1:3) = 'PAX' 
145563         IF (BBR-CHK = 80 AND BBR-BYTE (80) <= 'z' AND
                   BBR-BYTE (80) > SPACES) OR   
145563            (BBR-CHK = 81 AND BBR-BYTE (81) <= 'z' AND
                   BBR-BYTE (81) > SPACES) OR   
145563            (BBR-CHK = 82 AND BBR-BYTE (82) <= 'z' AND
                   BBR-BYTE (82) > SPACES) OR   
145563            (BBR-CHK = 83 AND BBR-BYTE (83) <= 'z' AND
                   BBR-BYTE (83) > SPACES) OR   
145563            (BBR-CHK = 84 AND BBR-BYTE (84) <= 'z' AND
                   BBR-BYTE (84) > SPACES)  

145563           GO TO 9700-FIX-IT-EXIT 
145563        END-IF
145563     END-IF.  

           IF  BBR-BYTE (BBR-CHK) NOT = '.' 
145563         IF  BBR-TYPE = 'SPNC'
145563             IF BBR-BYTE (BBR-CHK) > 'z'    OR
145563                BBR-BYTE (BBR-CHK) < SPACES   
145563                MOVE SPACES         TO BBR-BYTE (BBR-CHK) 
145563             END-IF   
145563         ELSE 
193641*            IF  BBR-BYTE (BBR-CHK) < ZERO OR 
193641             IF  BBR-BYTE (BBR-CHK) < SPACES OR   
142447*                BBR-BYTE (BBR-CHK) > 'Z' 
142447                 BBR-BYTE (BBR-CHK) > 'z' 
                       MOVE SPACES         TO BBR-BYTE (BBR-CHK)
                   END-IF   
145563         END-IF   
           END-IF.  

       9700-FIX-IT-EXIT.
            EXIT.   

       9750-CONT-FIX-IT.

142635*******  ADDED LOGIC HERE TO ALLOW NEGATIVE SIGN IN FILE FOR  
142635*******  EOR-ORDER-NET-DUE FIELD, WHICH NOW CAN HAVE A NON-ZERO   
142635*******  VALUE WHICH WILL BE NEGATIVE.
142635     IF BBR-CONT-CHK = 191 AND BBR-CONT-BYTE (191) = '-'  
142635        IF BIG-CONT-BUCKET-REC (1:3) = 'EOR'  
142635           GO TO 9750-CONT-EXIT   
142635        END-IF
142635     END-IF.  
142635  
           IF  BBR-CONT-BYTE (BBR-CONT-CHK) < ZERO OR   
142447*        BBR-CONT-BYTE (BBR-CONT-CHK) > 'Z'   
142447         BBR-CONT-BYTE (BBR-CONT-CHK) > 'z'   
               MOVE SPACES             TO BBR-CONT-BYTE (BBR-CONT-CHK)  
           END-IF.  

       9750-CONT-EXIT.  
            EXIT.   

       9800-PIAR-FIX-IT.

           PERFORM 9850-PIAR-MID-NAME-FIX-IT THRU   
                   9850-PIAR-MID-NAME-FIX-EXIT  
               VARYING PIAR-CHK FROM 1 BY 1 
               UNTIL PIAR-CHK > 20. 

       9800-PIAR-FIX-IT-EXIT.   
            EXIT.   

       9850-PIAR-MID-NAME-FIX-IT.   

           IF  WS-PIAR-PAX-MIDDLE-NAME-CHAR (PIAR-CHK) IS ALPHABETIC OR 
               WS-PIAR-PAX-MIDDLE-NAME-CHAR (PIAR-CHK) IS NUMERIC    OR 
               WS-PIAR-PAX-MIDDLE-NAME-CHAR (PIAR-CHK) = '-'         OR 
               WS-PIAR-PAX-MIDDLE-NAME-CHAR (PIAR-CHK) = "'"
               CONTINUE 
           ELSE 
               MOVE SPACES             TO WS-PIAR-PAX-MIDDLE-NAME   
               MOVE 21                 TO PIAR-CHK  
           END-IF.  

       9850-PIAR-MID-NAME-FIX-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

207857*       IF SSCIR-TABLE-ID (SSUB) = RSP06-IBR-SSCIR-TABLE-ID

207857*           MOVE SSCIR-TABLE-CNT TO SSUB  
P79185        END-IF
207857        IF (RSP06-IBR-SSCIR-DEBARK-TBL-ID > SPACES)           AND 
207857           (SSCIR-TABLE-ID (SSUB) =   
207857            RSP06-IBR-SSCIR-DEBARK-TBL-ID)                    AND

207857            MOVE SSCIR-END-TIME   (SSUB) TO WSPAX-CHKOUT-END-TIME 
207857        END-IF
